   6: --- Compute the repository root from this file's path.
   7: -- 莉取悽譁・ｻｶ霍ｯ蠕・耳蟇ｼ莉灘ｺ捺ｹ逶ｮ蠖輔・
   8: local function repoRoot()
   9:     -- __FILE__ is not available in all Lua versions; use a relative fallback.
  10:     -- busted is expected to be run from the repo root, so "." is sufficient.
  11:     return "."
  12: end
  13: M.repoRoot = repoRoot
  14: 
  15: --- Load a single addon file as WoW would: pass (addonName, NS) as varargs.
  16: -- 讓｡諡・WoW 蜉霓ｽ Lua 譁・ｻｶ逧・婿蠑擾ｼ悟ｰ・addonName 蜥・NS 菴應ｸｺ蜿ｯ蜿伜盾謨ｰ莨蜈･縲・
  17: ---@param path string  Relative path from repo root (e.g. "addon/Core/Init.lua")
  18: ---@param addonName string  Addon name string (e.g. "RotaAssist")
  19: ---@param ns table   Shared namespace table (NS)
  20: ---@return boolean ok, string|nil err
  21: function M.loadAddonFile(path, addonName, ns)
  22:     local fullPath = repoRoot() .. "/" .. path
  23:     local fn, err = loadfile(fullPath)
  24:     if not fn then
  25:         return false, "loadfile failed: " .. tostring(err)
  26:     end
  27:     -- Call with the WoW-style varargs so `local ADDON_NAME, NS = ...` works
  28:     local ok, loadErr = pcall(fn, addonName, ns)
  29:     if not ok then
  30:         return false, "execution failed: " .. tostring(loadErr)
  31:     end
  32:     return true, nil
  33: end
  34: 
  35: --- Build a fresh, minimal shared namespace table.
  36: -- 蛻帛ｻｺ荳荳ｪ蟷ｲ蜃逧・NS 蜻ｽ蜷咲ｩｺ髣ｴ陦ｨ縲・
  37: ---@return table ns
  38: function M.newNS()
  39:     return {}
  40: end
  41: 
  42: --- Load mock_wow_api.lua once before any addon files.
  43: -- 蝨ｨ蜉霓ｽ謠剃ｻｶ譁・ｻｶ蜑咲｡ｮ菫・WoW API mock 蟾ｲ蟆ｱ菴阪・
  44: local _mockLoaded = false
  45: function M.ensureMockLoaded()
  46:     if _mockLoaded then return end
  47:     local ok, err = M.loadAddonFile("tests/mock_wow_api.lua", "", {})
  48:     if not ok then error("Failed to load mock_wow_api: " .. tostring(err)) end
  49:     _mockLoaded = true
  50: end
  51: 
  52: --- Full addon loader: loads mock then Init.lua, returns (RA, NS).
  53: -- 蜉霓ｽ mock 蜷主・蜉霓ｽ Init.lua・瑚ｿ泌屓 (RA, NS)縲・
  54: ---@return table RA, table NS
  55: function M.loadAddon()

  59:     if not ok then error("loadAddon failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
  60:     return ns.RA, ns
  61: end

  65: ---@param ns table  Namespace table with ns.RA set

  69:     if not ok then error("loadRegistry failed: " .. tostring(err)) end

  74:     end

   1: -- RotaAssist WoW API Mock for busted unit tests
   2: -- WoW 謠剃ｻｶ蜊募・豬玖ｯ慕畑 API 讓｡諡溷ｱ・
   3: -- All globals that WoW addons expect are set here on _G.
   4: 
   5: -- ============================================================
   6: -- Table utilities (WoW builtins that may not exist in vanilla Lua)
   7: -- ============================================================
   8: 
   9: --- wipe(t): Clear all keys in table, return it (WoW global)
  10: function wipe(t)
  11:     for k in pairs(t) do t[k] = nil end
  12:     return t
  13: end
  14: _G.wipe = wipe
  15: 
  16: --- strsplit(delim, str, maxParts): split str by delim
  17: function strsplit(delim, str, maxParts)
  18:     local result = {}
  19:     local pattern = string.format("([^%s]+)", delim)
  20:     local count = 0
  21:     for part in str:gmatch(pattern) do
  22:         count = count + 1
  23:         result[count] = part
  24:         if maxParts and count >= maxParts then
  25:             -- Remainder goes into last slot as-is: re-join leftover
  26:             local consumed = table.concat(result, delim, 1, count - 1) .. delim
  27:             result[count] = str:sub(#consumed + 1)
  28:             break
  29:         end
  30:     end
  31:     return unpack(result)
  32: end
  33: _G.strsplit = strsplit

 -- Helpers

+---Reusable scratch set for iterModulesOrdered. Module-level so the ordered
+---walk allocates nothing per call (it runs twice on every OnEnable).
+---iterModulesOrdered 复用的临时集合；提到模块级避免每次调用都分配 table。
+---@type table<string, boolean>
+local orderVisited = {}
+
+---Modules already reported as missing from MODULE_ORDER, so the drift warning
+---is printed at most once per module per session.
+---已经报告过「不在 MODULE_ORDER 中」的模块，保证每个模块每会话只警告一次。
+---@type table<string, boolean>
+local orderDriftReported = {}
+
 ---Iterate modules in stable MODULE_ORDER, then any extras not in the list.
+---
+---Modules missing from MODULE_ORDER still run, but only AFTER every listed
+---module — including all UI modules — which silently breaks dependency order.
+---That drift is invisible today, so emit a one-shot debug warning per module.
+---不在 MODULE_ORDER 中的模块仍会执行，但会被排到所有已列模块（含全部 UI）之后，
+---静默破坏依赖顺序。此处对每个漂移模块打印一次 debug 警告，让问题自动暴露。
 ---@param callback function(name, mod)
 local function iterModulesOrdered(callback)
-    local visited = {}
+    wipe(orderVisited)
     for _, name in ipairs(MODULE_ORDER) do
         local mod = RA.modules[name]
         if mod then
-            visited[name] = true
+            orderVisited[name] = true
             callback(name, mod)
         end
     end
     -- Any module registered but NOT in the order list runs last
     for name, mod in pairs(RA.modules) do
-        if not visited[name] then
+        if not orderVisited[name] then
+            if not orderDriftReported[name] then
+                orderDriftReported[name] = true
+                RA:PrintDebug(string.format(
+                    "Module '%s' is not in MODULE_ORDER (Core/Init.lua) — it runs after every listed module. "
+                    .. "模块 '%s' 不在 MODULE_ORDER 中，会被排到所有已列模块之后。",
+                    name, name))
+            end
             callback(name, mod)
         end
     end

+---Whether a spell should be shown in the panel.

+---@param spellID number
+---@return boolean isTracked
+local function isSpellTracked(spellID)
+    local cdDb = RA.db and RA.db.profile and RA.db.profile.cooldowns
+    if cdDb and cdDb.trackedSpells then
+        local override = cdDb.trackedSpells[spellID]
+        if override ~= nil then
+            return override
+        end
+    end
+    return true
+end

 180: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 181: -- Runner
 182: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 183: 
 184: local counts = { pass = 0, fail = 0, pending = 0 }
 185: local failures = {}
 186: 
 187: local function pathOf(block, leaf)
 188:     local parts = {}
 189:     local b = block
 190:     while b and b.name ~= "" do
 191:         table.insert(parts, 1, b.name)
 192:         b = b.parent
 193:     end
 194:     parts[#parts + 1] = leaf
 195:     return table.concat(parts, " > ")
 196: end
 197: 
 198: local function chainBeforeEach(block, out)
 199:     if block.parent then chainBeforeEach(block.parent, out) end
 200:     for _, fn in ipairs(block.beforeEach) do out[#out + 1] = fn end
 201: end
 202: 
 203: local function chainAfterEach(block, out)
 204:     for _, fn in ipairs(block.afterEach) do out[#out + 1] = fn end
 205:     if block.parent then chainAfterEach(block.parent, out) end
 206: end
 207: 
 208: local function runTest(block, entry, fileLabel)
 209:     local pre, post = {}, {}
 210:     chainBeforeEach(block, pre)
 211:     chainAfterEach(block, post)
 212: 
 213:     local function body()
 214:         for _, fn in ipairs(pre) do fn() end
 215:         entry.fn()
 216:     end
 217: 
 218:     inRun = true
 219:     local ok, err = pcall(body)
 220:     inRun = false
 221:     -- after_each always runs, mirroring busted.
 222:     for _, fn in ipairs(post) do pcall(fn) end
 223: 
 224:     if ok then
 225:         counts.pass = counts.pass + 1
 226:     elseif err == PENDING_SENTINEL
 227:         or (type(err) == "table" and err.__pending) then
 228:         counts.pending = counts.pending + 1
 229:     else
 230:         counts.fail = counts.fail + 1
 231:         failures[#failures + 1] = {
 232:             file = fileLabel,
 233:             path = pathOf(block, entry.name),
 234:             err  = tostring(err),
 235:         }
 236:         io.write("F")
 237:         return
 238:     end
 239:     io.write(ok and "." or "P")
 240: end
 241: 
 242: local function runBlock(block, fileLabel)
 243:     for _, fn in ipairs(block.setups) do
 244:         local ok, err = pcall(fn)
 245:         if not ok then
 246:             counts.fail = counts.fail + 1
 247:             failures[#failures + 1] = {
 248:                 file = fileLabel,
 249:                 path = pathOf(block, "<setup>"),
 250:                 err  = tostring(err),
 251:             }
 252:             io.write("F")
 253:             return  -- setup failed: skip the whole block, same as busted
 254:         end
 255:     end
 256:     for _, entry in ipairs(block.children) do
 257:         if entry.kind == "it" then
 258:             runTest(block, entry, fileLabel)
 259:         elseif entry.kind == "pending" then
 260:             counts.pending = counts.pending + 1
 261:             io.write("P")
 262:         else
 263:             runBlock(entry.block, fileLabel)
 264:         end
 265:     end
 266:     for _, fn in ipairs(block.teardowns) do pcall(fn) end
 267: end
 268: 
 269: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 270: -- File discovery & main
 271: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 272: 
 273: local function listTestFiles()
 274:     local files = {}
 275:     -- Windows dir + POSIX ls fallback; sorted for deterministic order.
 276:     local h = io.popen('dir /b tests\\test_*.lua 2>nul') or io.popen('ls tests/test_*.lua 2>/dev/null')
 277:     if h then
 278:         for line in h:lines() do
 279:             local name = line:match("(test_[%w_]+%.lua)$")
 280:             if name then files[#files + 1] = "tests/" .. name end
 300: _G.pending     = dsl_pending
 301: _G.setup       = function(fn) reg(current.setups)(fn) end
 302: _G.teardown    = function(fn) reg(current.teardowns)(fn) end
 303: _G.before_each = function(fn) reg(current.beforeEach)(fn) end
 304: _G.after_each  = function(fn) reg(current.afterEach)(fn) end
 305: _G.assert      = assertShim
 306: 
 307: local started = os.clock()
 308: 
 309: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 310: -- File-level insulation, mirroring busted's default behavior.
 311: -- busted snapshots and restores the environment between test files; without
 312: -- this, a file that mutates mock state (e.g. swaps C_Spell.GetSpellCooldown)
 313: -- silently breaks later files 窶・an order dependency the CI runs never showed.
 314: -- 譁・ｻｶ郤ｧ髫皮ｦｻ・亥ｯｹ鮨・busted 鮟倩ｮ､陦御ｸｺ・会ｼ壽ｯ丈ｸｪ譁・ｻｶ蜷取△螟榊・螻邇ｯ蠅・ｹｶ蠑ｺ蛻ｶ驥崎ｽｽ
 315: -- mock・悟凄蛻吝燕髱｢譁・ｻｶ蟇ｹ mock 逧・隼蜉ｨ莨壽ｱ｡譟灘錘髱｢逧・枚莉ｶ・磯｡ｺ蠎丈ｾ晁ｵ厄ｼ峨・------------------------------------------------------------------------
 316: local function snapshotGlobals()
 317:     local snap = {}
 318:     for k, v in pairs(_G) do snap[k] = v end
 319:     return snap
 320: end
 321: 
 322: local function restoreGlobals(snap)
 323:     for k in pairs(_G) do
 324:         if snap[k] == nil then _G[k] = nil end
 325:     end
 326:     for k, v in pairs(snap) do _G[k] = v end
 327: end
 328: 
 329: local function resetModules()
 330:     -- Force tests.helpers (and its _mockLoaded upvalue) to reload next require,
 331:     -- which in turn reloads mock_wow_api.lua and rebuilds every mock API table.
 332:     for name in pairs(package.loaded) do
 333:         if name:match("^tests%.") then package.loaded[name] = nil end
 334:     end
 335: end
 336: 
 337: for _, file in ipairs(files) do
 338:     local keep = #filters == 0
 339:     for _, f in ipairs(filters) do
 340:         if file:find(f, 1, true) then keep = true break end
 341:     end
 342:     if keep then
 343:         -- Fresh subtree per file, insulated environment (matches busted).
 344:         local snap = snapshotGlobals()
 345:         resetModules()
 346:         root = newBlock("", nil)
 347:         current = root
 348:         io.write(file, " ")
 349:         local fn, loadErr = loadfile(file)
 350:         if not fn then
 351:             counts.fail = counts.fail + 1
 352:             failures[#failures + 1] = { file = file, path = "<loadfile>", err = tostring(loadErr) }
 353:             io.write("F\n")
 354:         else
 355:             local ok, err = pcall(fn)
 356:             if not ok then
 357:                 counts.fail = counts.fail + 1
 358:                 failures[#failures + 1] = { file = file, path = "<file body>", err = tostring(err) }
 359:                 io.write("F\n")
 360:             else
 361:                 runBlock(root, file)
 362:                 io.write("\n")
 363:             end
 364:         end
 365:         restoreGlobals(snap)
 366:     end
 367: end
 368: 
 369: local elapsed = os.clock() - started
 370: 
 371: print(string.rep("-", 60))
 372: print(string.format("%d passed / %d failed / %d pending  (%.2fs)",
 373:     counts.pass, counts.fail, counts.pending, elapsed))
 374: 
 375: if #failures > 0 then
 376:     print()
 377:     for i, f in ipairs(failures) do
 378:         print(string.format("FAIL %d) %s :: %s", i, f.file, f.path))
 379:         print("     " .. f.err:gsub("\n", "\n     "))
 380:     end
 381:     os.exit(1)
 382: end
 383: os.exit(0)

 160: ---Populate cooldown icon widgets from CooldownTracker's tracked spells.
 161: local function populateIcons()
 162:     if not container then return end
 163: 
 164:     -- Hide & release old widgets
 165:     for _, widget in pairs(cdWidgets) do
 166:         widget.frame:Hide()
 167:     end
 168:     cdWidgets = {}
 169: 
 170:     local cdTracker = RA:GetModule("CooldownTracker")
 171:     if not cdTracker then return end
 172: 
 173:     local allCD = cdTracker:GetAllCooldowns()
 174:     -- Sort by spellID for consistent ordering
 175:     local sortedIDs = {}
 176:     for spellID in pairs(allCD) do
 177:         sortedIDs[#sortedIDs + 1] = spellID
 178:     end
 179:     table.sort(sortedIDs)
 180: 
 181:     local index = 0
 182:     for _, spellID in ipairs(sortedIDs) do
 183:         if index >= MAX_ICONS then break end
 184:         if cdTracker:IsSpellTracked(spellID) then
 185:             index = index + 1
 186:             cdWidgets[spellID] = createCDWidget(spellID, index)
 187:         end
 188:     end
 189: 
 190:     -- Resize container to fit icons
 191:     if index > 0 then
 192:         local totalWidth = (ICON_SIZE * index) + (ICON_SPACING * (index - 1)) + 4
 193:         container:SetSize(totalWidth, ICON_SIZE + 4)
 194:     end
 195: end
 196: 
 197: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 198: -- Update Loop
 199: ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 200: 
 201: ---Update all cooldown icon states (text, desaturation, cooldown swirl).
 202: local function refreshCooldowns()
 203:     if not container or not isVisible then return end
 204: 
 205:     local cdTracker = RA:GetModule("CooldownTracker")
 206:     if not cdTracker then return end
 207: 
 208:     for spellID, widget in pairs(cdWidgets) do
 209:         local state = cdTracker:GetCooldownState(spellID)
 210:         if state then
 211:             if state.ready then
 212:                 widget.timeText:SetText("")
 213:                 widget:SetDesaturated(false)
 214:                 widget:SetAlert(false)
 215:                 widget.cooldown:Clear()

codex
The commit deletes modules that remain in the TOC and removes a tracker still consumed by CooldownPanel, causing load errors and a blank panel. The new mandatory test runner also contains several isolation and false-green defects.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Remove deleted modules from the TOC — C:/projects/RotaAssist/addon/Core/AssistCapture.lua:10-10
  For every addon package built from this commit, `addon/RotaAssist.toc:28-29` still lists both deleted Lua files, so WoW attempts to open nonexistent paths and reports load errors. Remove these entries and the stale `MODULE_ORDER` names in the same change.

- [P1] Migrate CooldownPanel before deleting CooldownTracker — C:/projects/RotaAssist/addon/Core/CooldownTracker.lua:10-10
  When the cooldown panel is enabled, its unchanged tooltip, `populateIcons`, and `refreshCooldowns` functions still request `RA:GetModule("CooldownTracker")`. After this deletion that lookup returns nil and `populateIcons` exits at `CooldownPanel.lua:171`, leaving the panel empty; migrate those consumers to `CooldownOverlay` or retain the tracker.

- [P2] Restore nested mutations during file isolation — C:/projects/RotaAssist/scripts/run_tests.lua:316-319
  When a spec mutates a field of a pre-existing global table, this shallow snapshot stores the same table reference and cannot restore the field. The existing `test_init_slash.lua:18-20` adds `string.trim`, which remains visible to every later file, so undeclared mock dependencies can pass despite the claimed file-level insulation.

- [P2] Propagate hook errors into the test result — C:/projects/RotaAssist/scripts/run_tests.lua:222-222
  When an `after_each` or `teardown` hook raises, including from an assertion, the discarded `pcall` result here and at line 266 lets the test remain counted as passed and the process can exit 0. Busted treats hook errors as failures, so record them while still running the remaining cleanup hooks.

- [P2] Fail when filters select no tests — C:/projects/RotaAssist/scripts/run_tests.lua:338-342
  When every supplied filter is misspelled or no longer matches a filename, this loop executes no files, the failure list stays empty, and the runner exits 0 with `0 passed / 0 failed`. Since filtered runs are documented as a pre-commit gate, track the selected count and return nonzero when filters select nothing.

- [P2] Run POSIX discovery when the Windows command fails — C:/projects/RotaAssist/scripts/run_tests.lua:276-276
  When this runner uses native Lua on Linux or macOS, `io.popen` still returns a non-nil pipe handle even though the Windows `dir` command fails, so the short-circuit `or` never invokes `ls` and no tests are discovered. Detect the platform or inspect the first command's result before attempting the fallback.

  When a spec mutates a field of a pre-existing global table, this shallow snapshot stores the same table reference and cannot restore the field. Since filtered runs are documented as a pre-commit gate, track the selected count and return nonzero when filters select nothing.

- [P2] Run POSIX discovery when the Windows command fails — C:/projects/RotaAssist/scripts/run_tests.lua:276-276
  When this runner uses native Lua on Linux or macOS, `io.popen` still returns a non-nil pipe handle even though the Windows `dir` command fails, so the short-circuit `or` never invokes `ls` and no tests are discovered. Detect the platform or inspect the first command's result before attempting the fallback.
